_source line 1437 · 2026-05-22T22:54:55.338716+00:00_

# ACL Rolling Review Form: Complete Scoring Dimensions

## 1. **Reviewer Confidence**
Scale: 1–5
- **5**: "Positive that my evaluation is correct. I read the paper very carefully and am familiar with related work."
- **4**: Quite sure with low likelihood of missing critical information
- **3**: Pretty sure, though some details may be unchecked
- **2**: Willing to defend evaluation, but likely missed some details
- **1**: Outside expertise area or paper very difficult to understand

## 2. **Soundness**
Scale: 1–5 (with .5 increments)
- **5**: "This study is one of the most thorough I have seen, given its type."
- **4**: Strong support for claims; extra experiments optional
- **3**: Sufficient support for main claims; minor details need attention
- **2**: Main claims lack support; major technical/methodological problems
- **1**: "Major Issues: This study is not yet sufficiently thorough to warrant publication"

## 3. **Excitement**
Scale: 1–5 (with .5 increments)
- **5**: Highly exciting; would recommend to others
- **4**: Exciting; would mention to peers
- **3**: Interesting; might mention some points
- **2**: Potentially interesting but doesn't resonate personally
- **1**: Not exciting; minimal community relevance

## 4. **Overall Assessment**
Scale: 1–5 (with .5 increments)
- **5**: Consider for Award (top 2.5%)
- **4**: Conference acceptance
- **3**: Accept to Findings
- **2**: Resubmit next cycle
- **1**: Do not resubmit

## 5. **Additional Dimensions**

**Reproducibility** (1–5): Can readers replicate results or build upon findings?

**Datasets** (1–5): Value of released datasets to research community

**Software** (1–5): Utility of released code to others

**Author Identity Knowledge** (1–5): Assessor's awareness of author identity

<details open><summary>🔧 Tool call — mcp__agent-ml-research__review_reviewer_post_to_director</summary>

```json
{
  "text": "## Reviewer 1 审稿意见 — 方法严谨性 + 实验设计 + 统计分析\n\n---\n\n## 1. Summary\n\n本文提出 episode-level counterfactual diagnostic framework，将 LLM agent routing signal 分解为 death avoidance 和 non-death 两个组成部分。在 ScienceWorld（11/30 task types, N=245 episodes）上发现 78.9% 的 non-tie routing labels 源自 death asymmetry（penalty-independent），DAF=90.4%（penalty-dependent at P=100）。训练的 router 在 episode-level 泛化上低于随机基线（47.1±2.2%）。跨模型（Llama-3.1-8B, Gemma-2-9B）和跨环境（MATH, ALFWorld）probes 提供初步支撑。核心贡献是诊断性的——在构建 learned router 之前应先检查 routing signal 是否被 catastrophic failure 所主导。\n\n---\n\n## 2. Strengths\n\n**S1. 新颖且有价值的诊断视角。** 现有 routing 工作直接训练 router 而不先理解 routing signal 的构成。本文首次系统分解 routing signal，填补了一个重要的方法论空白。DAF 的数学定义（§2.4, Eq. 5）清晰，penalty-independent label fraction（78.9%）作为补充指标的选择也合理。\n\n**S2. 极其详尽的统计分析。** 20 个 appendices（A–T）提供了罕见的深度：bootstrap CI、cluster-robust t-test、wild cluster bootstrap、exact permutation test、sign test、LOO correlation robustness（Table 5）、LOO DAF sensitivity（Table 7）、penalty sensitivity analysis（Table 12–13）。论文自觉标注了 K=11 cluster bootstrap 的局限性并推荐 non-clustered CI 作为 primary inference——这种统计自省在投稿论文中非常少见。\n\n**S3. 诚实的负面结果报告。** Router 训练的 episode-level failure（47.1±2.2%, below chance）被详细呈现而非掩盖。step-level vs. episode-level evaluation 的 generalization gap 分析（Table 10）、text augmentation ablation（Table 11）、capacity scaling（Table 18）系统地排除了 data scarcity 和 model capacity 作为唯一解释。这种透明度增加了论文可信度。\n\n**S4. Anti-cherry-picking 分析有说服力。** Table 4 显示 19 个被排除 task types 的 mean ΔDR=+38.2 pp 高于 included tasks 的 +28.7 pp。Extended validation（Appendix S, Table 21, n=20 per task）进一步确认 excluded tasks 的 DAF（0.991）高于 included tasks（0.904）。这有效排除了选择偏差的质疑。\n\n**S5. 跨模型复现。** 三个 7–9B 模型家族（Qwen2.5-7B, Llama-3.1-8B, Gemma-2-9B-it）在 ScienceWorld 上一致显示 death dominance，directional consistency 100%（10/10 death-prone tasks），internal vulnerability 近乎一致（~42% vs ~41% vs ~36%）。Table 17 的 consolidation table 是有效的 robustness evidence。\n\n**S6. Algorithm 1 设计合理且假设明确。** Death-priority rule（lines 5–10）的有效条件——P 超过 maximum partial score——被明确标注（Assumption note, p.13）。ScienceWorld 满足此条件（P=100, max partial=100）。Labeling Quality Audit（Appendix B）验证了 zero tie-breaking labels，95 个训练 labels 全部 genuine。\n\n**S7. Failure Mode Analysis 增加 mechanistic depth。** Appendix N 揭示 100% internal deaths 由同一 action pattern（`focus on [object]`）触发，提供了 actionable 的 mechanistic insight。Grounding ablation（Appendix O）量化了 prompt warning（-11 pp）和 action-space filtering（-19 pp）的效果。\n\n---\n\n## 3. Weaknesses\n\n**W1 [Major] DAF 的 headline metric 对 penalty 的数学依赖性削弱了主要发现的 robustness。**\n- 具体位置：§2.4 Eq. 5, Table 12（Appendix K）\n- 问题：DAF = δP/(δP + Δ_nd) 是 P 的单调递增函数。Abstract 和 Introduction 的 headline 数字 DAF=90.4% 是在 P=100 下计算的。Table 12 显示在 P=0 时 DAF=7.4%，P=10 时 50.4%，P=25 时 70.7%。论文确实讨论了这一依赖性（§2.4 \"DAF varies monotonically with P\"；Appendix K \"this is a mathematical consequence\"），但 abstract/introduction 仍以 DAF=90.4% 为主导叙事，penalty-independent label fraction（78.9%）被置于次要位置。\n- 影响：读者可能对 death avoidance 的 dominance 程度产生过度印象。DAF 的值取决于 ScienceWorld 的 specific reward structure（P=100 是环境默认值，不是理论上必然的），在其他 penalty structure 下 death avoidance 的 dominance 可能大幅降低。\n- 建议：在 abstract/introduction 中将 penalty-independent label fraction（78.9%）作为 primary metric，DAF=90.4% 作为 penalty-specific supplement。明确标注 \"at ScienceWorld's default P=100\"。\n\n**W2 [Major] 63% task exclusion rate 的 justification 虽合理但对 generalizability 有实质限制。**\n- 具体位置：§3 第1段, Table 3–4（Appendix C）\n- 问题：19/30 task types 被排除。三个排除类别（dev set 5, redundancy 7, agent floor 7）各自合理，但累积效应是分析仅基于 ScienceWorld 的 37% task coverage。\"Agent capability floor\" 排除的 7 个 tasks 的 task progress 为 near-zero，但这可能与 30-step budget 限制有关——论文承认 \"increasing the step budget could surface non-death-dominated signal\"（Limitations §1）。\"Taxonomically redundant\" 排除的 7 个 tasks 的判断标准不完全透明——如何定义 \"family representative\" 和 redundancy？\n- 影响：虽然 Table 4 的 anti-cherry-picking analysis 有效排除了选择偏差（excluded tasks 更 death-dominant），但 37% coverage 限制了对 ScienceWorld 整体的 generalizability claim。\n- 建议：明确 taxonomic redundancy 的判断标准（e.g., 共享 reward function structure？）。对 agent-floor tasks，报告在更长 step budget（e.g., 50, 100 steps）下是否出现 non-death routing signal。\n\n**W3 [Major] Router negative result 可能是极端 class imbalance（9/245=3.7% internal-preferred）的 artifact，而非 routing signal 本身的 limitation。**\n- 具体位置：§3.6, Table 10–11（Appendix J）\n- 问题：在 245 episodes 中仅 9 个 internal-preferred（3.7%）。任何分类器在此 imbalance 下都倾向于 majority-class collapse。Text augmentation ablation（Table 11）仅做 paraphrase-based augmentation，不增加 genuine episodes。论文承认 \"genuine episode-level scaling (collecting more counterfactual pairs) remains an open question\"，但随即估算需要 ~1,350 paired episodes（5.5× expansion）便放弃探索。\n- 影响：Router failure 被呈现为 \"signal-limited\" 结论（§3.6），但实际上 data-limited 的假设未被充分排除。在 3.7% minority rate 下，signal-limited vs. data-limited 是 underdetermined 的。\n- 建议：(1) 明确标注 router failure 是 \"under the current data regime (N=245, 3.7% minority)\" 而非 \"routing signal does not exist for learned routers in general\"；(2) 讨论 selective collection strategy（只收集 death-free episodes）是否能提高 minority rate 以更公平地测试 router。\n\n**W4 [Major] 跨环境 probes 的 \"deterministic\" computation mode 在三个环境中语义不同，限制了 cross-environment comparison 的 validity。**\n- 具体位置：§3.4, Table 14（Appendix L）\n- 问题：ScienceWorld deterministic = oracle simulator forking；MATH deterministic = Python code interpreter；ALFWorld deterministic = admissible-action enumeration。这三种 \"deterministic\" computation modes 在信息特权程度、计算成本和 failure mode 上完全不同。论文 Table 14 footnote 承认了这一点（\"deterministic denotes qualitatively different computation modes\"），但仍将三个环境的结果并列讨论并声称框架具有 cross-environment generality。\n- 影响：MATH 的 \"no routing signal\" finding 不能直接与 ScienceWorld 的 \"death-dominated signal\" 对比，因为 code interpreter 和 oracle simulator 是不同类型的外部工具。ALFWorld 的 information asymmetry finding 是一个独立发现，但不直接验证 ScienceWorld 的 death dominance 机制。\n- 建议：更谨慎地框定 cross-environment 结果：强调框架（counterfactual decomposition procedure）是通用的，而非具体 findings 可跨环境迁移。\n\n**W5 [Minor] Cohen's d=3.02（Table 6, Appendix E）的解释需要更多 context。**\n- 具体位置：Appendix E, Table 6\n- 问题：d=3.02 是在 K=11 task-type clusters 上计算的。10/11 clusters 显示同方向（唯一例外是 zero-death task），directional consistency 近乎完美。此 effect size 虽然 numerically large，但更多反映 binary structure（death-prone tasks 几乎一致表现出 death asymmetry）而非 continuous effect 的 magnitude。\n- 建议：加一句解释 d=3.02 reflects near-binary directional consistency rather than a graded effect magnitude。\n\n**W6 [Minor] 单一 death mechanism（`focus on`）限制了 failure mode analysis 的 generalizability。**\n- 具体位置：Appendix N\n- 问题：所有 105 个 internal deaths 由同一 action pattern 触发。这是 ScienceWorld 特有的 API 设计问题（answer-submission action 被误用为 exploration action）。如果换一个 action space 设计（e.g., 将 `focus on` 从 exploration phase 移除），death rate 可能大幅降低。Grounding ablation（Appendix O）的 action-space filtering 已证实这一点（-19 pp）。\n- 影响：论文的 general claim（\"any environment with asymmetric fatal outcomes\"）基于一个非常特定的 failure mechanism，generalizability 存疑。\n- 建议：在 Discussion 中更明确区分 ScienceWorld-specific 的 `focus on` failure mode 和 general death avoidance framework 的适用范围。\n\n**W7 [Minor] 72B scaling probe 的 scope 过窄。**\n- 具体位置：§3.3, Table 1\n- 问题：仅 Qwen2.5-72B 一个模型，没有 cross-model replication at scale（论文在 Limitations 中承认）。Death rate 从 42.9% 降至 30.6%，death dominance persists（DAF=92.4%），但这是单点数据，无法建立 scaling trend。N=255 episodes 是合理的，但仅一个 72B 模型的 evidence 不足以讨论 \"scaling\" implications。\n- 建议：将 72B 结果定位为 \"preliminary probe\" 而非 scaling evidence；或增加 Llama-3.1-70B 作为 cross-model 验证。\n\n---\n\n## 4. Questions\n\n**Q1.** Algorithm 1 的 death-priority rule 在 both-dead 情况下赋予 `no-pref` label（line 10），不区分 \"both die at step 5\" vs \"both die at step 25\"。在时间维度上是否丢失了有用的 routing signal？\n\n**Q2.** Table 3 中 `find-non-living-thing` 的 n=40（两批收集），而其他 tasks n=20。这是否引入了 estimation precision 的不对称？为什么不对所有 tasks 收集 n=40？\n\n**Q3.** Reflexion 实验（§3.3, Figure 3）在 N=3 后 death rate stabilizes at ~35%。为什么不测试 N>5？Is there a theoretical reason to expect no further improvement, or is this a computational budget constraint?\n\n**Q4.** The practitioner decision framework（Appendix R, Table 20）的 thresholds（15 pp, 75%, 50%）仅有 4 个 retrospective data points 的 validation。这些 thresholds 的选择依据是什么？是否有 sensitivity analysis showing robustness to threshold perturbation？\n\n**Q5.** DAF 定义中 Δ_nd = 2.84 是在 ScienceWorld 的 11 task types 上测量的 non-death score gap。这个值的 bootstrap CI 是多少？如果 Δ_nd 的 uncertainty 较大，DAF 的 interpretability 是否受影响？\n\n---\n\n## 5. Missing References\n\n- **Safe RL literature**: 论文讨论 catastrophic failure 但未引用 safe reinforcement learning 的核心工作（e.g., Garcıa & Fernández 2015 已引用，但缺少 constrained MDP 如 Altman 1999 的引用——已包含于 references，这点收回）。\n- **Routing/mixture-of-experts 在 inference-time 的工作**: 论文引用了 RouteLLM（Ong et al., 2024）和 CogRouter，但缺少 Hybrid LLM routing 的更早期工作如 **FrugalGPT (Chen et al., 2023)** 和 **AutoMix (Madaan et al., 2023)**，这些工作也涉及 cost-quality tradeoff routing。\n- **Step-level routing 的 foundational work**: Options framework (Sutton et al., 1999) 或 hierarchical RL 中 temporal abstraction 的经典文献可能与 episode-level vs. step-level routing 的讨论相关。\n\n---\n\n## 6. 格式检查\n\n- **正文页数**：8 页（p.1–p.8），符合 EMNLP 2026 long paper 8-page 限制。\n- **Limitations**：存在，位于正文之后（p.9），覆盖 5 个方面（environmental scope, methodological constraints, statistical limitations, pure-strategy upper bound, diagnostic scope），内容充分。\n- **Ethics Statement**：存在（p.10），覆盖数据来源、计算资源、代码发布计划。\n- **匿名合规**：作者为 \"Anonymous ACL submission\"，PDF 中未发现去匿名化信息。`\\hypersetup{pdfauthor={},pdftitle={}}` 已清空 PDF metadata。\n- **`\\usepackage[review]{acl}`**：已使用。\n- **参考文献格式**：使用 `\\bibliography{references}`，格式统一。\n- **Responsible NLP Checklist**：未在 PDF 中出现，符合规定（在 OpenReview 表单中填写）。\n- **无合规问题。**\n\n---\n\n## 7. 官方维度打分（ACL ARR Review Form）\n\n| 维度 | 分数 | 理由摘要 |\n|------|------|----------|\n| **Soundness** | 3.5/5 | 在 ScienceWorld 内部的分析极其充分（bootstrap CI, cluster-robust, LOO, penalty sensitivity），但 DAF 对 P 的数学依赖（W1）和 router failure 的 class imbalance confound（W3）削弱了两个核心 claim 的 robustness。跨环境 generalizability 受限于不同 \"deterministic\" mode 的不可比性（W4）。 |\n| **Excitement** | 3.5/5 | \"先诊断再构建\" 的理念对 agent routing 社区有实质价值。但 findings 高度 environment-specific（单一 death mechanism），prescriptive value 有限（decision framework 仅 4 个 retrospective validation points）。框架的理论贡献（counterfactual decomposition）比具体实证 findings 更有持久价值。 |\n| **Reproducibility** | 4/5 | Algorithm 1 明确，所有超参数公开（Table 16），prompt templates 完整提供（Appendix T），模型均为公开权重。但代码和数据 \"upon acceptance\" 发布，当前不可复现。 |\n| **Confidence** | 4/5 | 我仔细阅读了全部 24 页（含 20 个 appendices），熟悉 agent routing、counterfactual inference 和 bootstrap 统计方法。对 ScienceWorld 环境的 domain-specific 细节不如 specialist 熟悉。 |\n\n---\n\n## 8. Overall Assessment\n\n**Overall: 3.5 — Borderline Conference**\n\n**一句话总结：** 一篇方法论上极其严谨的诊断性论文，提出了有价值的 counterfactual decomposition framework 并发现 death avoidance 主导 ScienceWorld routing signal，但 findings 的 generalizability 受限于单一环境的特定 death mechanism、DAF 对 penalty 的数学依赖、以及 router negative result 的 class imbalance confound。\n\n**详细判断依据：**\n\n*接收方向的论据*：(1) 框架新颖，填补了 \"先诊断再构建 router\" 的方法论空白；(2) 统计分析的深度和透明度在 agent routing 领域罕见；(3) 负面结果的诚实报告（router failure）对社区有警示价值；(4) 跨模型复现（3 families）增强了 ScienceWorld 内部发现的可信度。\n\n*拒绝方向的论据*：(1) 核心 findings 与 ScienceWorld 的特定 death mechanism 紧密耦合，框架的 general utility 未被跨环境实证充分支持（W4）；(2) DAF=90.4% 的 headline 对 P 的数学依赖使 dominance 程度的 interpretation 不够 robust（W1）；(3) Router failure 的 \"signal-limited\" 结论在 3.7% minority rate 下 underdetermined（W3）；(4) 63% task exclusion 虽有合理 justification 但限制了 coverage（W2）。\n\n总体而言，论文的方法论贡献（diagnostic framework）足以进入 Findings；如果 W1（DAF framing）和 W3（router failure interpretation）能在 camera-ready 中 addressed，Conference 接收也合理。",

Summary\n\n本文提出 episode-level counterfactual diagnostic framework，将 LLM agent routing signal 分解为 death avoidance 和 non-death 两个组成部分。在 ScienceWorld（11/30 task types, N=245 episodes）上发现 78.9% 的 non-tie routing labels 源自 death asymmetry（penalty-independent），DAF=90.4%（penalty-dependent at P=100）。训练的 router 在 episode-level 泛化上低于随机基线（47.1±2.2%）。跨模型（Llama-3.1-8B, Gemma-2-9B）和跨环境（MATH, ALFWorld）probes 提供初步支撑。核心贡献是诊断性的——在构建 learned router 之前应先检查 routing signal 是否被 catastrophic failure 所主导。\n\n---\n\n## 2. Strengths\n\n**S1. 新颖且有价值的诊断视角。** 现有 routing 工作直接训练 router 而不先理解 routing signal 的构成。本文首次系统分解 routing signal，填补了一个重要的方法论空白。DAF 的数学定义（§2.4, Eq. 5）清晰，penalty-independent label fraction（78.9%）作为补充指标的选择也合理。\n\n**S2. 极其详尽的统计分析。** 20 个 appendices（A–T）提供了罕见的深度：bootstrap CI、cluster-robust t-test、wild cluster bootstrap、exact permutation test、sign test、LOO correlation robustness（Table 5）、LOO DAF sensitivity（Table 7）、penalty sensitivity analysis（Table 12–13）。论文自觉标注了 K=11 cluster bootstrap 的局限性并推荐 non-clustered CI 作为 primary inference——这种统计自省在投稿论文中非常少见。\n\n**S3. 诚实的负面结果报告。** Router 训练的 episode-level failure（47.1±2.2%, below chance）被详细呈现而非掩盖。step-level vs. episode-level evaluation 的 generalization gap 分析（Table 10）、text augmentation ablation（Table 11）、capacity scaling（Table 18）系统地排除了 data scarcity 和 model capacity 作为唯一解释。这种透明度增加了论文可信度。\n\n**S4. Anti-cherry-picking 分析有说服力。** Table 4 显示 19 个被排除 task types 的 mean ΔDR=+38.2 pp 高于 included tasks 的 +28.7 pp。Extended validation（Appendix S, Table 21, n=20 per task）进一步确认 excluded tasks 的 DAF（0.991）高于 included tasks（0.904）。这有效排除了选择偏差的质疑。\n\n**S5. 跨模型复现。** 三个 7–9B 模型家族（Qwen2.5-7B, Llama-3.1-8B, Gemma-2-9B-it）在 ScienceWorld 上一致显示 death dominance，directional consistency 100%（10/10 death-prone tasks），internal vulnerability 近乎一致（~42% vs ~41% vs ~36%）。Table 17 的 consolidation table 是有效的 robustness evidence。\n\n**S6. Algorithm 1 设计合理且假设明确。** Death-priority rule（lines 5–10）的有效条件——P 超过 maximum partial score——被明确标注（Assumption note, p.13）。ScienceWorld 满足此条件（P=100, max partial=100）。Labeling Quality Audit（Appendix B）验证了 zero tie-breaking labels，95 个训练 labels 全部 genuine。\n\n**S7. Failure Mode Analysis 增加 mechanistic depth。** Appendix N 揭示 100% internal deaths 由同一 action pattern（`focus on [object]`）触发，提供了 actionable 的 mechanistic insight。Grounding ablation（Appendix O）量化了 prompt warning（-11 pp）和 action-space filtering（-19 pp）的效果。\n\n---\n\n## 3. headline 数字 DAF=90.4% 是在 P=100 下计算的。Table 12 显示在 P=0 时 DAF=7.4%，P=10 时 50.4%，P=25 时 70.7%。论文确实讨论了这一依赖性（§2.4 \"DAF varies monotonically with P\"；Appendix K \"this is a mathematical consequence\"），但 abstract/introduction 仍以 DAF=90.4% 为主导叙事，penalty-independent label fraction（78.9%）被置于次要位置。\n- 影响：读者可能对 death avoidance 的 dominance 程度产生过度印象。DAF 的值取决于 ScienceWorld 的 specific reward structure（P=100 _source line 1445 · 2026-05-22T23:01:01.964895+00:00_

[Round 1 审稿请求]:
以下是 Reviewer 2 的完整审稿意见，请逐条回应：对每条 Weakness 标注 [AGREE] 同意并说明理由，或 [REBUT] 反驳并提供证据（cite 具体 Section/Table/Figure）。

---

## Reviewer 2 审稿意见（完整原文）

### Summary
本文提出 episode-level counterfactual 框架诊断 LLM agent routing 信号。在 ScienceWorld 中发现 78.9% 非平局 routing 标签源于 death avoidance，提出 DAF 和 SCI 指标，覆盖 3 个 7–9B 模型家族和 MATH/ALFWorld 跨环境验证。

### Strengths
S1. 问题定位精准：diagnostic vs. prescriptive routing 的差异化清晰，Section 5 中的 practitioner guidance（DAF≈0 → always-deterministic）有实用价值。
S2. 实验严谨：episode-level bootstrap (B=10,000)、3 模型家族 cross-model replication、4 intervention axes、sensitivity analysis (Appendix K)。
S3. 方法论有独立价值：Algorithm 1 + DAF/SCI 可复用，不依赖特定发现。
S4. Appendix 极详尽（16 页，A–T），可复现性高。
S5. 跨环境 probes 虽初步但 hedging 语言诚实（"preliminary evidence"）。

### Weaknesses
W1. [Critical] 正文超 8 页。Sections 1–6 约 8.5 页（Discussion 延至 p.9 上半，Conclusion 结束于 p.9 中部）。EMNLP 2026 限 8 页，可能 desk reject。建议压缩 Section 4/5 或将 Figure 2 移至 appendix。

W2. [Major] 核心发现 triviality 风险。ScienceWorld 的 −100 death penalty 使 death-asymmetric episodes 自然携带不成比例的 score gap。DAF 随 P 单调递增是 mathematical consequence（Appendix K 自认）。论文需正面区分 "DAF 值 (penalty-dependent)" 和 "label fraction 78.9% (penalty-invariant)"，并论证后者的非平凡性。

W3. [Major] Figure 2 近似恒等关系占正文空间。r=0.97 的 scatter plot 是 DAF≈1 的可视化重述，作者自己说 "not an independent finding"。应移至 appendix。

W4. [Major] Non-death component（21.1%）分析不足。78.9% death avoidance 之外的 routing signal 来自什么？SCI 显示集中在 top-3 task types 但无 mechanistic 解释。需 case study。

W5. [Minor] 跨环境验证 scope 与泛化 claim 有张力。MATH 和 ALFWorld 各仅 5-6 task types。Discussion 中 "framework's generality claim is methodological" 需更多证据或更谨慎措辞。

W6. [Minor] 数字密度过高。几乎每段 3-5 个带 CI 的数字，Abstract 尤其严重。建议将 secondary statistics 移至 footnotes/appendix。

W7. [Minor] Related Work 缺 non-agent adaptive computation。未覆盖 Mixture-of-Depths、speculative decoding 等共享动态计算分配思想的方向。

W8. [Minor] Introduction 与 Conclusion contributions 列表近乎逐字重复。页数超限下应削减冗余。

### Questions
Q1. DAF 值 (90.4%) vs label fraction (78.9%) 的区别和各自解读？这对 triviality 论证关键。
Q2. 19 个 excluded task types 是无信号还是样本不足？
Q3. Section 3.6 Trained Router 的 routing decision accuracy 是多少？
Q4. Router 使用什么特征？能否直接学到 "death-prone → deterministic" 映射？
Q5. "no prior work has decomposed routing decisions in interactive environments" 的 novelty claim 是否过强？

### Missing References
- Mixture-of-Depths (Raposo et al., 2024)
- Speculative decoding (Leviathan et al., 2023; Chen et al., 2023)
- LATS (Zhou et al., 2024)
- Trial and Error (Wang et al., 2024)

### Scoring
Soundness: 3.5/5
Excitement: 2.5/5
Reproducibility: 4/5

### Overall: 3.0 — Findings
### Confidence: 4/5

primary/secondary 定位。\n\n**Q2 (excluded tasks):** 我在 W2 中已讨论。Table 4（Appendix C）的 extended prescreening（Group B/C 各 n=5）和 Appendix S（n=20 extended validation）提供了合理 evidence，但 \"agent floor\" tasks page 9 上方，之后才进入 Limitations。主文（Sections 1–6）约 8.3–8.5 页，超出 EMNLP 2026 的 8 页限制。R2 建议的 \"压缩 Section 4/5 或移 Figure 2 至 appendix\" 是可操作的。实际上 Section 4 Related Work 和 Section 5 Discussion 之间存在内容重叠（e.g., Discussion 中对 routing failure 的 mechanistic explanation 与 Related Work 中 safe RL 的讨论有交集），合并或压缩可行。这是 camera-ready 前必须修复的硬约束。\n\n---\n\n### W2 [Major] 核心发现 triviality 风险\n\n**[AGREE] R2-W2:** 与我的 W1 一致。DAF=90.4% 是 P=100 下的数学结果，Table 12（Appendix K）明确展示了 DAF 从 P=0 的 7.4% 到 P=500 的 97.9% 的单调递增。论文自身在 Appendix K 承认 \"this is a mathematical consequence of the decomposition (DAF = δP/(δP + Δ_nd)), not evidence of mechanism robustness\"。\n\n但需注意 R2 可能低估了 penalty-independent label fraction（78.9%）的非平凡性。78.9% 意味着：在完全不考虑 penalty 数值的情况下，仅看 binary death indicator（一方死亡、另一方存活），就已经解释了近 4/5 的 routing labels。这个指标不依赖 P，是纯粹的 event-level 统计。论文在 §3.1 和 §2.4 都讨论了两个指标的关系（\"The label fraction, invariant to penalty magnitude, serves as the primary finding\"），但 abstract/introduction 的叙事结构确实以 DAF=90.4% 为主导，需要调整优先级。\n\n建议作者在 abstract 中将 \"78.9% of non-tie routing labels arise from death asymmetry\" 作为 headline finding，DAF=90.4% 作为 \"at ScienceWorld's default penalty\" 的补充。\n\n---\n\n### W3 [Major] Figure 2 近似恒等关系\n\n**[REBUT] R2-W3:** 部分不同意将此定级为 Major。\n\nR2 的论点是正确的：r=0.97 scatter plot 是 DAF≈1 的可视化重述，论文自己在 Figure 2 caption 中承认 \"the near-perfect correlation is a structural consequence of death dominance, not an independent finding (§3.1)\"。在页数超限（W1）的前提下，移至 appendix 是合理的空间优化。\n\n但 Figure 2 有不可替代的 pedagogical 功能：它是论文 decomposition framework 的唯一可视化入口——读者通过这张图直觉理解 \"per-task death rate differential 几乎完全解释 per-task score gap\"。如果移除，Section 3.1 的核心论证将完全依赖 inline 数字（r=0.97, Spearman ρ=0.986），对非统计背景的读者不够直观。\n\n建议：保留 Figure 2 但缩小尺寸（从 full-column 缩至 half-column），释放约 1/4 页空间用于解决 W1 的页数问题。这比完全移除的信息损失更小。\n\n---\n\n### W4 [Major] Non-death component（21.1%）分析不足\n\n**[AGREE] R2-W4:** 同意这是论文的一个显著缺口。\n\n78.9% death-driven labels 之外，剩余 21.1%（=20 个 score-comparison labels + 一些 death-free episodes 的 label）的 routing signal 来源未得到充分解释。具体而言：\n\n1. **20 个 score-comparison labels**（Table 3, Appendix B）中 16 个 det-preferred、4 个 int-preferred，全部来自 both-survived episodes。论文仅报告 \"score-comparison labels contribute negligible additional signal\"（Appendix B），但未分析这 20 个 episode 中 deterministic 策略的 score advantage 来自什么——是 action grounding quality？exploration efficiency？task-specific domain knowledge？\n\n2. **`find-non-living-thing`** 是唯一 zero-death task，贡献了全部 20 个 score-comparison episodes。Table 3 显示其 Δ_full=+9.58，但论文未 deep-dive 分析这个 task 中两种 strategies 的 qualitative 差异。\n\n3. **SCI=0.2147**（Appendix A）表明 routing signal 相对分散（低 Gini coefficient），但论文称 \"top-3 task types account for only 30.8% of total death-rate differential\"——这说明 non-death signal 分布较均匀，但不解释 signal 的 mechanism。\n\n一个简短的 case study（e.g., 分析 2-3 个 score-comparison episode 的 trajectory differences）即可大幅改善。\n\n---\n\n### W5 [Minor] 跨环境验证 scope 与泛化 claim 有张力\n\n**[AGREE] R2-W5:** 与我的 W4 一致。三个环境的 \"deterministic\" computation mode 语义完全不同（§3.4, Table 14）：ScienceWorld=oracle simulator forking, MATH=code interpreter, ALFWorld=admissible-action enumeration。这限制了 cross-environment findings 的直接可比性。\n\n但需公正指出：论文在 Limitations（§Environmental scope）中明确写道 \"the framework's generality claim is methodological (the diagnostic procedure applies to any environment with asymmetric fatal outcomes), not empirical (the specific percentages may differ across environments)\"。这个 hedging 是到位的。R2 要求 \"更多证据或更谨慎措辞\"——我认为措辞已经足够谨慎，关键缺失的是更多同质环境的验证（e.g., 另一个有 death mechanism 的环境，如 NetHack 或 Crafter）。\n\n---\n\n### W6 [Minor] 数字密度过高\n\n**[REBUT] R2-W6:** 部分不同意。\n\n对于一篇诊断性/分析性论文，precise quantification 是核心贡献的载体。如果将关键数字移至 footnotes/appendix，正文的 claim 将缺乏 inline evidence。例如 Abstract 中的 \"78.9% of non-tie episode-level routing labels arise from death asymmetry\" 和 \"DAF reaches 90.4% (95% CI [81.1%, 99.0%])\" 是不可省略的——前者是 penalty-independent headline，后者提供 uncertainty quantification。\n\n但 R2 有一个合理的 point：某些 secondary statistics 可以压缩。例如 Abstract 中 \"a finding replicated across three architecturally distinct 7–9B model families (spread 2.2 pp)\" 的 \"spread 2.2 pp\" 可以移至正文。Section 3.1 中同时报告 bootstrap CI、Spearman ρ、LOO range 的 inline presentation 可以精简为保留 bootstrap CI，其余指向 appendix。\n\n---\n\n### W7 [Minor] Related Work 缺 non-agent adaptive computation\n\n**[AGREE] R2-W7:** 同意 Mixture-of-Depths（Raposo et al., 2024）和 speculative decoding（Leviathan et al., 2023; Chen et al., 2023）是相关的 adaptive computation 工作。虽然它们在 token-level 而非 episode-level 操作，但共享 \"动态分配计算资源\" 的核心思想。论文的 Related Work（Section 4）覆盖了 \"Tool Use and External Computation\"、\"Counterfactual Evaluation in RL\"、\"LLM Agent Environments\"、\"Safe RL and Causal Grounding\"、\"Adaptive Computation and Routing\" 五个类别，最后一个类别（p.7–8）讨论了 RouteLLM、CogRouter、Mixture-of-Experts，但确实缺少 non-agent 的 adaptive depth/speculation 文献。一段简短的对比（\"unlike token-level adaptive computation, our framework operates at the episode-level granularity\"）即可。\n\nLATS（Zhou et al., 2024）和 Trial and Error（Wang et al., 2024）是否遗漏需要进一步确认——论文引用了大量 agent 文献，可能以不同名称引用。\n\n---\n\n### W8 [Minor] Introduction 与 Conclusion contributions 列表重复\n\n**[AGREE] R2-W8:** 同意。在页数超限（W1）的情况下，这种冗余尤其需要消除。Conclusion 的 contributions list 可以压缩为 1–2 句 forward-looking summary，将具体 contributions 仅保留在 Introduction。这可释放约 1/4 页空间。\n\n---\n\n### 对 R2 Questions 的补充意见\n\n**Q1 (DAF vs label fraction):** 我在 W1 中已详细讨论。两个指标的区别是关键：label fraction 78.9% 是 penalty-invariant 的 event-level 统计，DAF 90.4% 是 penalty-dependent 的 score-weighted 指标。论文应更清晰地 primary/secondary 定位。\n\n**Q2 (excluded tasks):** 我在 W2 中已讨论。Table 4（Appendix C）的 extended prescreening（Group B/C 各 n=5）和 Appendix S（n=20 extended validation）提供了合理 evidence，但 \"agent floor\" tasks 的排除可能与 step budget 有关（Limitations 承认），需进一步探索。\n\n**Q3 (Trained Router accuracy):** 论文 §3.6 和 Appendix J 报告了详细数字：step-level 54.1±5.4%，episode-level 47.1±2.2%（below 50% chance）。End-to-end deployment（p.7）显示 router 将 97.0% episodes 分配给 deterministic，death rate 17.6%。\n\n**Q4 (Router features):** 论文 Appendix I 说明 router 使用 step-level observation text 作为输入（SFT + LoRA on Qwen3-1.7B）。Leave-one-task-type-out evaluation（Appendix P）显示 blind routing collapses to always-deterministic，keyword-based variant achieves 62.9% only through task-identity leakage。这表明 router 无法学到 generalizable routing features，只能学到 task-type identity。\n\n**Q5 (Novelty claim):** \"no prior work has decomposed routing decisions in interactive environments\" — 我认为这个 claim 基本成立但措辞可以更精确。现有 counterfactual evaluation 工作（Goyéniche et al., 2025; Gyevnár et al., 2025）在 agent 层面做 counterfactual analysis，但不针对 routing signal decomposition。建议修改为 \"no prior work has systematically decomposed what drives routing preferences in interactive agent environments\"。\n\n---\n\n### 对 R2 整体评分的评论\n\nR2 给出 Overall 3.0（Findings），主要 concerns 是 triviality risk（W2）和 page limit（W1）。我的评估略高（3.5, Borderline Conference），原因是：\n1. R2 的 Excitement 2.5 偏低——framework 的方法论价值超越了 ScienceWorld-specific findings，\"先诊断再构建 router\" 的理念对整个 agent routing 社区有指导意义。\n2. W1（page limit）是 formatting issue，不影响学术贡献评估。\n3. W2（triviality）被 78.9% penalty-independent label fraction 部分化解——这个数字不是 P 的函数。\n\n但我同意 R2 的 W4（non-death component 分析不足）是一个 substantive gap，降低了论文的 completeness。",

[Round 1 审稿请求]:
你是 Reviewer 1，负责**方法严谨性 + 实验设计**。

## 审稿论文
- 标题: "Death Avoidance as the Dominant Routing Signal: A Counterfactual Analysis on ScienceWorld"
- 论文路径: `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_12/projects/simgate-modular-routing/docs/paper/main.tex`
- PDF: `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_12/projects/simgate-modular-routing/artifacts/review_round_30/submitted.pdf`
- 目标 venue: **EMNLP 2026**（通过 ARR May 2026 cycle）
- 投稿类别: Long Paper
- Deadline: 2026-05-25

## Venue 要求
- 正文 8 页严格限制，references 无限制
- Limitations 章节必须有（不计入页数限制，但不能含过多额外内容）
- Ethical considerations 可选（不计入页数）
- Double-blind 审稿（作者信息匿名）
- ACL 模板
- ARR Responsible NLP Checklist 在 OpenReview submission form 中填写（不在论文 PDF 中）

## Overall Assessment 评分标准（EMNLP 2026 ARR）
5 = Consider for Award（约前2.5%），4.5 = Borderline Award，4 = Conference（可被主会议接收），3.5 = Borderline Conference，3 = Findings（可被Findings接收），2.5 = Borderline Findings，2 = Resubmit next cycle，1.5 = Resubmit after next cycle，1 = Do not resubmit

## 你的审查侧重
1. **方法论严谨性**：counterfactual framework 的有效性、DAF 分解的数学合理性、episode-level labeling 的假设和局限
2. **实验设计**：ScienceWorld 11/30 task types 的选择偏差问题、cross-model (Llama/Gemma/72B) 验证的充分性、router 训练实验的 confounds
3. **统计分析**：bootstrap CI 可靠性（K=11 cluster 问题）、effect size 报告、multiple comparison 处理
4. **可重复性**：实验描述是否充分、超参数报告是否完整
5. **格式合规**：8 页限制、匿名性、模板使用

## 输出格式
请按以下结构输出审稿意见：
```
## Summary (2-3 句)

## Strengths (逐条，标注 S1, S2, ...)

## Weaknesses (逐条，标注 W1, W2, ...，每条标注 severity: critical/warning/info)
- 对每个 weakness 给出具体证据（引用 Section/Table/Figure）

## Questions (逐条，Q1, Q2, ...)

## Missing References

## Overall Assessment
- Score: [用 EMNLP 2026 ARR 评分标准]
- Confidence: [1-5]
- Excitement: [1-5]
- Soundness: [1-5]
- 一句话总评
```

39	\paragraph{Environmental scope.}
40	Our primary analysis uses a single environment (ScienceWorld, 11 of 30 task types) with one backbone (Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct), though extended validation of representative excluded tasks confirms the pattern (Appendix~\ref{app:extended-validation}). The $-100$ death penalty amplifies DAF; environments with softer penalties would exhibit lower DAF values (Appendix~\ref{app:penalty}). The 19 excluded task types (63\%) lack routing signal because both strategies typically time out within 30 steps; increasing the step budget could surface non-death-dominated signal that our current setup does not capture. Cross-environment analyses on MATH and ALFWorld (\S\ref{sec:cross-env}, \S\ref{sec:discussion}) provide preliminary supporting evidence but do not conclusively establish generalization. _source line 1483 · 2026-05-23T03:36:09.631645+00:00_

# ACL Rolling Review Form - Complete Scoring Criteria

## 1. Reviewer Confidence
Scale: 1-5
- **5**: "Positive that my evaluation is correct. I read the paper very carefully and am familiar with related work."
- **4**: "Quite sure. I tried to check the important points carefully."
- **3**: "Pretty sure, but there's a chance I missed something."
- **2**: "Willing to defend my evaluation, but it is fairly likely that I missed some details."
- **1**: "Not my area, or paper is very hard to understand."

## 2. Soundness
Scale: 1-5 (with half-point increments)
- **5**: "Excellent: This study is one of the most thorough I have seen."
- **4**: "Strong: This study provides sufficient support for all of its claims."
- **3**: "Acceptable: This study provides sufficient support for its main claims."
- **2**: "Poor: Some of the main claims are not sufficiently supported."
- **1**: "Major Issues: This study is not yet sufficiently thorough to warrant publication."

## 3. Excitement
Scale: 1-5 (with half-point increments)
- **5**: "Highly Exciting: I would recommend this paper to others and/or attend its presentation."
- **4**: "Exciting: I would mention this paper to others and/or make an effort to attend."
- **3**: "Interesting: I might mention some points of this paper to others."
- **2**: "Potentially Interesting: this paper does not resonate with me, but it might with others."
- **1**: "Not Exciting: this paper does not resonate with me."

## 4. Overall Assessment
Scale: 1-5 (with half-point increments)
- **5**: Consider for Award (top 2.5% papers)
- **4.5**: Borderline Award
- **4**: Conference acceptance
- **3.5**: Borderline Conference
- **3**: Findings acceptance
- **2.5**: Borderline Findings
- **2**: Resubmit next cycle
- **1.5**: Resubmit after next cycle
- **1**: Do not resubmit

## 5. Reproducibility
Scale: 1-5
- **5**: Results easily reproducible
- **4**: Mostly reproducible with minor variations
- **3**: Reproducible with some difficulty
- **2**: Hard to reproduce;
…[truncated 724 chars]

_source line 1492 · 2026-05-23T03:36:27.689761+00:00_

1	\begin{abstract}
2	LLM agent routing (deciding when to rely on internal reasoning versus external tool use) is typically addressed by training routers directly, without first understanding what mechanistic signal drives the routing preference. This absence of diagnostic methods risks building routers atop confounded or trivial signal. We propose an episode-level counterfactual framework that compares pure-strategy outcomes (internal-only vs.\ deterministic-only) to decompose routing signal into its mechanistic components before any router is constructed. Applying this framework to ScienceWorld (11 task types, 245 episodes), we find that 78.9\% of non-tie episode-level routing labels arise from death asymmetry---a finding replicated across three architecturally distinct 7--9B model families, revealing that in catastrophic-failure environments, signal diversity can collapse to a single dominant mechanism. Cross-environment probes on MATH and ALFWorld diagnose qualitatively different routing mechanisms (no extractable signal and information asymmetry, respectively), providing preliminary evidence of applicability beyond death-dominated settings. The framework distinguishes \emph{methodological generality} (applicable to any environment with paired episode outcomes) from \emph{empirical generality} (full quantitative validation on ScienceWorld only). A ${\sim}$50-episode pilot suffices for build-vs-buy decisions given large effect sizes ($d = 3.02$).
3	\end{abstract}
4

1	\section{Introduction}
2	
3	Large language models deployed as interactive agents must repeatedly decide \emph{how} to compute: reason internally, or delegate to an external tool.
4	In ScienceWorld \citep{scienceworld-2022}, a text-based science simulation, internal reasoning is error-prone, as the model may hallucinate state changes (e.g., that water has boiled when the stove was never activated), while deterministic external computation eliminates such errors via exact state transitions. The practical question: \emph{when should an agent route computation externally versus internally?}
5	
6	Existing routing approaches frame this as a capability or efficiency decision: Toolformer \citep{toolformer-2023} inserts API calls by perplexity reduction; ART \citep{art-2023} decomposes tasks into tool-augmented programs; Chameleon \citep{chameleon-2023} selects heterogeneous modules per subtask; adaptive-computation methods route to different reasoning depths \citep{cogrouter-2026,ares-2026}, model tiers \citep{mtrouter-2026}, or episode-level model selection under sparse rewards \citep{budget-aware-routing-2026}; concurrent analyses further presuppose diverse routing signals~\citep{when2tool-2026,tool-use-tax-2026,to-call-or-not-2026}. These approaches, which operate in non-catastrophic settings, share the assumption that routing signal stems from diverse sources and a learned router can exploit it---an assumption that remains untested: no prior work has systematically decomposed what drives routing preferences in interactive environments.
7	
8	We propose a \emph{diagnostic framework} comprising (i)~episode-level counterfactual labeling under pure strategies (\S\ref{sec:collection}--\ref{sec:labeling}), and (ii)~the Death Avoidance Factor (DAF), which decomposes the routing score gap into death-avoidance and non-death components (\S\ref{sec:daf}). Applied to ScienceWorld, \textbf{78.9\% of non-tie routing labels arise from death asymmetry} (75 of 95), a penalty-independen
…[truncated 2187 chars]

1	\section{Related Work}
2	
3	\paragraph{Tool Use and External Computation in LLMs.}
4	A growing body of work augments language models with external tools \citep{tool-learning-survey-2024}, spanning several functional categories.
5	Self-supervised methods learn tool invocation from loss signals \citep{toolformer-2023,toolace-2025}, token-level methods embed tools in the vocabulary \citep{toolkengpt-2023}, task-level decomposition routes subtasks to specialized modules \citep{art-2023,chameleon-2023}, and RL-based methods learn routing from reward \citep{retool-2025}.
6	All focus on \emph{learning to use tools}; we ask a prior question: \emph{what signal drives the routing decision?}
7	Concurrent work examines \emph{when} to invoke tools: tool-call decisions correlate with uncertainty~\citep{tool-overuse-illusion-2026}, are encoded in hidden states~\citep{when2tool-2026}, impose a ``tool-use tax''~\citep{tool-use-tax-2026}, or follow necessity-utility-affordability logic~\citep{to-call-or-not-2026}. Our decomposition yields a concrete answer: 78.9\% of non-tie routing labels trace to catastrophic-failure prevention.
8	
9	\paragraph{Adaptive Computation and Routing.}
10	Adaptive routing traces back to learned halting policies \citep{graves2016act}. Mixture-of-Recursions \citep{mixture-of-recursions-2025} learns token-level recursion depths in the LLM era; concurrent work further diversifies routing granularity: CogRouter \citep{cogrouter-2026} adapts per-step depth, ARES \citep{ares-2026} selects effort levels, and MTRouter \citep{mtrouter-2026} addresses multi-turn settings; in death-dominated environments, the task-type-level binary signal is structurally inaccessible to these step-level approaches (\S\ref{sec:router-diagnostic}).
11	RouteLLM \citep{routellm-2024} learns routers from preference data to select between strong and weak models; cost-optimization routing (FrugalGPT~\citep{frugalgpt-2023}; AutoMix~\citep{automix-2024}) cascades queries across model tiers.

1	\section{Method}
2	\label{sec:method}
3	
4	\begin{figure}[t]
5	\centering
6	\includegraphics[width=\columnwidth]{figures/pipeline_overview.pdf}
7	\caption{Counterfactual episode collection and analysis pipeline. Each task instance is run under both pure strategies independently, producing episode-level labels via Algorithm~\ref{alg:labeling}.}
8	\label{fig:pipeline}
9	\end{figure}
10	
11	Our diagnostic framework quantifies what drives routing signal in interactive environments through three components: (i)~episode-level counterfactual labeling that generates routing preferences from paired pure-strategy outcomes (\S\ref{sec:collection}--\ref{sec:labeling}); (ii)~the Death Avoidance Factor (DAF), which decomposes the routing score gap into death-avoidance and non-death components (\S\ref{sec:daf}); and (iii)~the Signal Concentration Index (SCI), which measures whether routing signal is concentrated in a few task types or distributed systematically (Appendix~\ref{app:sci}). Episode-level bootstrap resampling provides 95\% confidence intervals for all metrics.
12	
13	\subsection{Problem Setup}
14	\label{sec:problem-setup}
15	
16	Consider an interactive environment with observation space $\mathcal{O}$ and action space $\mathcal{A}$. An agent policy $\pi: \mathcal{O}^* \rightarrow \mathcal{A}$ maps observation histories to actions. We define two computation modes $\mathcal{M} = \{{\tt int}, {\tt det}\}$: \textbf{internal} (${\tt int}$), where the agent predicts intermediate observations via chain-of-thought reasoning (ReAct~\citep{react-2023}), with prediction errors compounding across steps; and \textbf{deterministic} (${\tt det}$), a privileged-information strategy that eliminates observation uncertainty---instantiated as oracle simulator (ScienceWorld), code interpreter (MATH), or admissible-action enumeration (ALFWorld); these qualitative differences affect what routing signals the framework can detect. A \emph{routing policy} $\rho: \mathcal{O}^* \rightarrow \math

1	\section{Experiments}
2	\label{sec:experiments}
3	
4	We analyze 11 of ScienceWorld's 30 task types (245 episodes); the 19 excluded types (63\%) lack actionable routing signal due to development contamination (5), taxonomic redundancy (7), or agent-floor performance (7). Taxonomically redundant tasks share identical action vocabularies, reward functions, and environmental dynamics, differing only in target entity; we retain one representative per family (Appendix~\ref{app:per-task}). This scope limits generalizability: conclusions apply to the included subset; increasing the 30-step budget might surface signal in excluded types (see Limitations). An anti-cherry-picking analysis confirms that death dominance holds in 18 of these 19 types, with a mean $\Delta\text{DR} = +38.2$\,pp exceeding the included tasks' $+28.7$\,pp (Appendix~\ref{app:per-task}). Extended validation at $n{=}20$ on five representative excluded tasks confirms this direction ($\overline{\Delta\text{DR}} = +32.0$\,pp; Appendix~\ref{app:extended-validation}). A reasoning-depth ablation ($n{=}120$) found no significant difference between internal strategy variants ($p > 0.26$; Appendix~\ref{app:ablations}). Prescreening used $n{=}5$ episodes per task type; directional consistency (18/19 types favoring deterministic) rather than point estimates drives the inclusion criterion.
5	
6	\subsection{Death Avoidance Decomposition}
7	\label{sec:death-decomp}
8	
9	
10	Of the 95 non-tie routing labels, 75 (78.9\%) arise from death asymmetry: the internal strategy triggers a catastrophic $-100$ penalty while the deterministic strategy survives. This penalty-independent label fraction is the primary diagnostic metric. Of these, 54 are sole-signal labels where death was the only discriminator; at $P{=}0$ these reduce to ties, but the labeling function itself is penalty-independent (Algorithm~\ref{alg:labeling}).
11

1	\section{Discussion}
2	\label{sec:discussion}
3	
4	\paragraph{Signal granularity and router failure.}
5	The router diagnostic (\S\ref{sec:router-diagnostic}) reveals a structural mismatch: death avoidance is a task-type-level binary property (10/11 types carry death risk), but step-level features correlate weakly because death risk depends on multi-step trajectories. Six router variants (0.6B--4B, classification and generative) converge on near-chance step-level accuracy ($56$--$59\%$); this convergent failure is consistent with signal limitation; however, extreme class imbalance (90.5\% deterministic-preferred, only 9 internal-preferred episodes) makes a data-limited explanation equally plausible---distinguishing the two requires ${\sim}$1{,}350 paired episodes to obtain 50 minority-class samples. Cross-task-type transfer yields chance accuracy (49.2\%; Appendix~\ref{app:ablations}). A train--deploy distribution shift~\citep{dagger-2011} may also contribute to the negative result.
6	
7	\paragraph{Non-triviality and oracle confound.}
8	While death dominance follows from the penalty structure (\S\ref{sec:daf}), two consequences were not predictable a priori: $10{\times}$ scaling to 72B fails to eliminate it (DAF${}= 92.4\%$), and Reflexion yields DAF${}>100\%$. All four interventions leave ${\geq}12$~pp residual gaps (Appendix~\ref{sec:threshold-routing}); cross-model DR stability ($36$--$43\%$) confirms death vulnerability is a task-environment property. An oracle confound (``is the signal merely use the oracle?'') is addressed by two observations: $\Delta_{\text{nd}} = 2.84$ (9.6\% of total gap)---internal reasoning provides almost no non-death advantage---and action-space filtering (Appendix~\ref{app:grounding-ablation}) reduces death rate by 19~pp while remaining far above deterministic safety.
9	
10	\paragraph{Practical implications and actionability.}
11	The penalty-invariant label fraction (78.9\%) is the primary diagnostic; DAF varies with $P$ (Appendix~\re
…[truncated 671 chars]

1	\section{Conclusion}
2	
3	Our episode-level counterfactual framework reveals that 78.9\% of non-tie routing labels in ScienceWorld stem from death avoidance---replicated across three model families, persistent under $10{\times}$ scaling, and transferable to qualitatively different environments. Future work should validate this in additional catastrophic-failure settings and investigate lightweight death-prediction mechanisms that exploit task-type-level binary structure without full step-level router training.
4

Here are the complete contents of all the paper section files:

## abstract.tex

The abstract describes an episode-level counterfactual framework for understanding LLM agent routing decisions. The key finding is that 78.9% of non-tie routing labels in ScienceWorld arise from death asymmetry (catastrophic failures). The framework decomposes routing signal before constructing routers, testing the applicability across different environments (ScienceWorld, MATH, ALFWorld) and revealing that signal diversity collapses to death-avoidance as a single dominant mechanism in catastrophic-failure environments.

## introduction.tex

The introduction motivates the problem: LLM agents must decide whether to use internal reasoning or delegate to external tools. Existing routing approaches assume diverse routing signals, but this assumption remains untested. The paper proposes a diagnostic framework using episode-level counterfactual labeling and the Death Avoidance Factor (DAF) metric. Key findings include: 78.9% of non-tie routing labels stem from death asymmetry, replicated across multiple model families (Qwen2.5-7B, Llama-3.1-8B). The framework overcomes per-step oracle degeneracy (97.4% tie rate) and provides build-vs-buy signal from ~50 episodes.

## related_work.tex

This section positions the work within several research areas:
- **Tool use in LLMs**: Distinguishes the paper's focus on *what* signal drives routing from existing work on *learning to use* tools
- **Adaptive computation and routing**: Contrasts with step-level routing approaches and cost-optimization methods
- **Counterfactual evaluation in RL**: Notes the paper executes complete episodes rather than perturbing individual actions
- **LLM agent environments**: References ScienceWorld, ALFWorld, ReAct, Reflexion, and LATS
- **Safe RL and causal grounding**: Connects death avoidance to constrained MDPs and safe RL, using simplified potential outcomes framework

## method.tex

[Round 1 审稿请求]:
## Round 1 审稿任务 — Reviewer 1

### Venue 信息
- **目标**: EMNLP 2026 via ACL Rolling Review (ARR) May 2026 cycle
- **Deadline**: 2026-05-25 (ARR submission)
- **类型**: Long paper, 正文严格 8 页限制，references 无限，Limitations 和 Ethics 不计入页数
- **模板**: ACL style (`\usepackage[review]{acl}`)
- **匿名**: Double-blind (`\author{Anonymous}`)
- **评分标准**: Overall Assessment 5=Award, 4.5=Borderline Award, 4=Conference, 3.5=Borderline Conference, 3=Findings, 2.5=Borderline Findings, 2=Resubmit next cycle, 1.5=Resubmit after next cycle, 1=Do not resubmit

### 论文文件
- LaTeX 源码: `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_12/projects/simgate-modular-routing/docs/paper/main.tex`（入口文件，通过 `\input{}` 引入各章节）
- PDF: `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_12/projects/simgate-modular-routing/artifacts/review_round_30/submitted.pdf`
- 各章节 .tex 文件在 `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_12/projects/simgate-modular-routing/docs/paper/` 目录下：abstract.tex, introduction.tex, method.tex, experiments.tex, related_work.tex, discussion.tex, conclusion.tex
- Appendices 在 main.tex 中（A-U 共 21 个 appendix sections）

### 你的审查侧重：方法严谨性 + 实验设计 + 统计有效性

请重点审查：

1. **方法论严谨性**
   - Episode-level counterfactual labeling 的设计是否合理？Algorithm 1 的 death-priority rule 是否引入 bias？
   - DAF 指标定义是否 sound？DAF 对 penalty P 的单调依赖是否被充分讨论？
   - 78.9% label fraction vs DAF=90.4% 的关系是否清晰？哪个是 primary metric？
   - Pure-strategy comparison 的因果推断逻辑是否 valid？是否存在未讨论的 confound？

2. **实验设计**
   - 11/30 task types 的 prescreening 是否引入 selection bias？排除标准是否合理？
   - N=245 episodes 的 sample size 是否足够？statistical power 如何？
   - Cross-model validation (3 families, 7-9B) 的设计是否 convincing？72B scaling 是否 sufficient？
   - Agent improvement baselines (Retry, Reflexion) 的设计是否公平？对照组设置是否合理？
   - Cross-environment probes (MATH, ALFWorld) 的设计是否能支撑 generalization claims？
   - Router training diagnostic 的实验设计是否完整？negative result 的解释是否 fair？

3. **统计推断**
   - Bootstrap CI 的计算是否正确？episode-level resampling 的合理性？
   - Cluster bootstrap (K=11) 的可靠性？K<20 的问题是否被充分 caveat？
   - Cohen's d = 3.02 的解读是否需要额外 context？
   - Per-task analysis 的 statistical power (n=20) 是否足够？

4. **格式合规**
   - 正文是否严格在 8 页以内（Sections 1-6）？如果超出，标注为 CRITICAL
   - 是否符合 ACL 模板要求？

### 输出格式

请按以下结构输出完整审稿意见（必须包含所有 section）：

```
## Summary
（2-3 句论文概述）

## Strengths
S1. ...
S2. ...
...

## Weaknesses
W1. [severity: critical/warning/info] ...
W2. ...
...

## Questions
Q1. ...
...

## Missing References
（如有遗漏的重要相关工作）

## Scores
- Soundness: X/5
- Excitement: X/5
- Overall: X (使用 EMNLP 2026 评分标准)
- Confidence: X/5

## Detailed Comments
（逐节详细意见）
```

**请直接输出完整审稿意见，不要只说"已读完"。**

ARR developed detailed guidelines and suggestions for each step of the reviewing process, including a list of common problems in both reviews and NLP papers to watch out for.

The reviewer and AC guidelines include codes for (meta-)review issues (I1-I12) that can be reported by the authors. These issue codes help standardize common problems in the review process.

_source line 1539 · 2026-05-23T03:44:27.819488+00:00_

# ACL Rolling Review Form Dimensions and Scoring

Based on the reviewer guidelines provided, here's what I found regarding the review form structure:

## Primary Scoring Dimensions

The guidelines indicate ARR uses **three main scores** as of February 2025:

1. **Soundness** - assesses technical validity and rigor
2. **Excitement** - reflects personal interest and novelty appreciation
3. **Overall Recommendation** - composite judgment for venue acceptance

## Scoring Guidance

**Soundness:** The guidelines state that "soundness scores must be justified by the text of the review." Low soundness without documented faults indicates misalignment between review and recommendation.

**Excitement:** Described as "orthogonal to the assessment of soundness" and reflecting "personal taste" that "do not necessarily come with explicit reasons."

**Overall Assessment:** A "composite score reflecting your assessment of soundness, excitement, and also other factors like novelty and impact."

## Important Limitation

**The document does not provide the specific numerical scales** (e.g., whether each dimension uses 1-5, 1-7, or other ranges). The guidelines reference a separate "ARR review form" post and OpenReview tutorials for detailed scoring mechanics, but those specific rubric definitions are not included in this content.

For complete scoring scales and level definitions, reviewers are directed to the referenced "[ARR review form](https://aclrollingreview.org/reviewform)" resource.

_source line 1554 · 2026-05-23T03:45:05.675621+00:00_

# ACL Rolling Review Form - Complete Scoring Dimensions

## 1. Reviewer Confidence
- **5**: "Positive that my evaluation is correct. I read the paper very carefully and am familiar with related work."
- **4**: "Quite sure. I tried to check the important points carefully. It's unlikely, though conceivable, that I missed something."
- **3**: "Pretty sure, but there's a chance I missed something. Although I have a good feel for this area in general, I did not carefully check the paper's details."
- **2**: "Willing to defend my evaluation, but it is fairly likely that I missed some details, didn't understand some central points."
- **1**: "Not my area, or paper is very hard to understand. My evaluation is just an educated guess."

## 2. Soundness
- **5**: "Excellent: This study is one of the most thorough I have seen, given its type."
- **4**: "Strong: This study provides sufficient support for all of its claims. Some extra experiments could be nice, but not essential."
- **3**: "Acceptable: This study provides sufficient support for its main claims. Some minor points may need extra support or details."
- **2**: "Poor: Some of the main claims are not sufficiently supported. There are major technical/methodological problems."

- **1**: "Not Exciting: this paper does not resonate with me, and I don't think it would with others in the *ACL community."

1	\section{Method}
2	\label{sec:method}
3	
4	\begin{figure}[t]
5	\centering
6	\includegraphics[width=\columnwidth]{figures/pipeline_overview.pdf}
7	\caption{Counterfactual episode collection and analysis pipeline. Each task instance is run under both pure strategies independently, producing episode-level labels via Algorithm~\ref{alg:labeling}.}
8	\label{fig:pipeline}
9	\end{figure}
10	
11	Our diagnostic framework quantifies what drives routing signal in interactive environments through three components: (i)~episode-level counterfactual labeling that generates routing preferences from paired pure-strategy outcomes (\S\ref{sec:collection}--\ref{sec:labeling}); (ii)~the Death Avoidance Factor (DAF), which decomposes the routing score gap into death-avoidance and non-death components (\S\ref{sec:daf}); and (iii)~the Signal Concentration Index (SCI), which measures whether routing signal is concentrated in a few task types or distributed systematically (Appendix~\ref{app:sci}). Episode-level bootstrap resampling provides 95\% confidence intervals for all metrics.
12	
13	\subsection{Problem Setup}
14	\label{sec:problem-setup}
15	
16	Consider an interactive environment with observation space $\mathcal{O}$ and action space $\mathcal{A}$. An agent policy $\pi: \mathcal{O}^* \rightarrow \mathcal{A}$ maps observation histories to actions. We define two computation modes $\mathcal{M} = \{{\tt int}, {\tt det}\}$: \textbf{internal} (${\tt int}$), where the agent predicts intermediate observations via chain-of-thought reasoning (ReAct~\citep{react-2023}), with prediction errors compounding across steps; and \textbf{deterministic} (${\tt det}$), a privileged-information strategy that eliminates observation uncertainty---instantiated as oracle simulator (ScienceWorld), code interpreter (MATH), or admissible-action enumeration (ALFWorld); these qualitative differences affect what routing signals the framework can detect. A \emph{routing policy} $\rho: \mathcal{O}^* \rightarrow \math

1	\section{Experiments}
2	\label{sec:experiments}
3	
4	We analyze 11 of ScienceWorld's 30 task types (245 episodes); the 19 excluded types (63\%) lack actionable routing signal due to development contamination (5), taxonomic redundancy (7), or agent-floor performance (7). Taxonomically redundant tasks share identical action vocabularies, reward functions, and environmental dynamics, differing only in target entity; we retain one representative per family (Appendix~\ref{app:per-task}). This scope limits generalizability: conclusions apply to the included subset; increasing the 30-step budget might surface signal in excluded types (see Limitations). An anti-cherry-picking analysis confirms that death dominance holds in 18 of these 19 types, with a mean $\Delta\text{DR} = +38.2$\,pp exceeding the included tasks' $+28.7$\,pp (Appendix~\ref{app:per-task}). Extended validation at $n{=}20$ on five representative excluded tasks confirms this direction ($\overline{\Delta\text{DR}} = +32.0$\,pp; Appendix~\ref{app:extended-validation}). A reasoning-depth ablation ($n{=}120$) found no significant difference between internal strategy variants ($p > 0.26$; Appendix~\ref{app:ablations}). Prescreening used $n{=}5$ episodes per task type; directional consistency (18/19 types favoring deterministic) rather than point estimates drives the inclusion criterion.
5	
6	\subsection{Death Avoidance Decomposition}
7	\label{sec:death-decomp}
8	
9	
10	Of the 95 non-tie routing labels, 75 (78.9\%) arise from death asymmetry: the internal strategy triggers a catastrophic $-100$ penalty while the deterministic strategy survives. This penalty-independent label fraction is the primary diagnostic metric. Of these, 54 are sole-signal labels where death was the only discriminator; at $P{=}0$ these reduce to ties, but the labeling function itself is penalty-independent (Algorithm~\ref{alg:labeling}).

1	\section{Discussion}
2	\label{sec:discussion}
3	
4	\paragraph{Signal granularity and router failure.}
5	The router diagnostic (\S\ref{sec:router-diagnostic}) reveals a structural mismatch: death avoidance is a task-type-level binary property (10/11 types carry death risk), but step-level features correlate weakly because death risk depends on multi-step trajectories. Six router variants (0.6B--4B, classification and generative) converge on near-chance step-level accuracy ($56$--$59\%$); this convergent failure is consistent with signal limitation; however, extreme class imbalance (90.5\% deterministic-preferred, only 9 internal-preferred episodes) makes a data-limited explanation equally plausible---distinguishing the two requires ${\sim}$1{,}350 paired episodes to obtain 50 minority-class samples. Cross-task-type transfer yields chance accuracy (49.2\%; Appendix~\ref{app:ablations}). A train--deploy distribution shift~\citep{dagger-2011} may also contribute to the negative result.
6	
7	\paragraph{Non-triviality and oracle confound.}
8	While death dominance follows from the penalty structure (\S\ref{sec:daf}), two consequences were not predictable a priori: $10{\times}$ scaling to 72B fails to eliminate it (DAF${}= 92.4\%$), and Reflexion yields DAF${}>100\%$. All four interventions leave ${\geq}12$~pp residual gaps (Appendix~\ref{sec:threshold-routing}); cross-model DR stability ($36$--$43\%$) confirms death vulnerability is a task-environment property. An oracle confound (``is the signal merely use the oracle?'') is addressed by two observations: $\Delta_{\text{nd}} = 2.84$ (9.6\% of total gap)---internal reasoning provides almost no non-death advantage---and action-space filtering (Appendix~\ref{app:grounding-ablation}) reduces death rate by 19~pp while remaining far above deterministic safety.
9	
10	\paragraph{Practical implications and actionability.}
11	The penalty-invariant label fraction (78.9\%) is the primary diagnostic; DAF varies with $P$ (Appendix~\re

1	\section{Introduction}
2	
3	Large language models deployed as interactive agents must repeatedly decide \emph{how} to compute: reason internally, or delegate to an external tool.
4	In ScienceWorld \citep{scienceworld-2022}, a text-based science simulation, internal reasoning is error-prone, as the model may hallucinate state changes (e.g., that water has boiled when the stove was never activated), while deterministic external computation eliminates such errors via exact state transitions. The practical question: \emph{when should an agent route computation externally versus internally?}
5	
6	Existing routing approaches frame this as a capability or efficiency decision: Toolformer \citep{toolformer-2023} inserts API calls by perplexity reduction; ART \citep{art-2023} decomposes tasks into tool-augmented programs; Chameleon \citep{chameleon-2023} selects heterogeneous modules per subtask; adaptive-computation methods route to different reasoning depths \citep{cogrouter-2026,ares-2026}, model tiers \citep{mtrouter-2026}, or episode-level model selection under sparse rewards \citep{budget-aware-routing-2026}; concurrent analyses further presuppose diverse routing signals~\citep{when2tool-2026,tool-use-tax-2026,to-call-or-not-2026}. These approaches, which operate in non-catastrophic settings, share the assumption that routing signal stems from diverse sources and a learned router can exploit it---an assumption that remains untested: no prior work has systematically decomposed what drives routing preferences in interactive environments.
7	
8	We propose a \emph{diagnostic framework} comprising (i)~episode-level counterfactual labeling under pure strategies (\S\ref{sec:collection}--\ref{sec:labeling}), and (ii)~the Death Avoidance Factor (DAF), which decomposes the routing score gap into death-avoidance and non-death components (\S\ref{sec:daf}). Applied to ScienceWorld, \textbf{78.9\% of non-tie routing labels arise from death asymmetry} (75 of 95), a penalty-independen
…[truncated 2187 chars]

1	\section{Related Work}
2	
3	\paragraph{Tool Use and External Computation in LLMs.}
4	A growing body of work augments language models with external tools \citep{tool-learning-survey-2024}, spanning several functional categories.
5	Self-supervised methods learn tool invocation from loss signals \citep{toolformer-2023,toolace-2025}, token-level methods embed tools in the vocabulary \citep{toolkengpt-2023}, task-level decomposition routes subtasks to specialized modules \citep{art-2023,chameleon-2023}, and RL-based methods learn routing from reward \citep{retool-2025}.
6	All focus on \emph{learning to use tools}; we ask a prior question: \emph{what signal drives the routing decision?}
7	Concurrent work examines \emph{when} to invoke tools: tool-call decisions correlate with uncertainty~\citep{tool-overuse-illusion-2026}, are encoded in hidden states~\citep{when2tool-2026}, impose a ``tool-use tax''~\citep{tool-use-tax-2026}, or follow necessity-utility-affordability logic~\citep{to-call-or-not-2026}. Our decomposition yields a concrete answer: 78.9\% of non-tie routing labels trace to catastrophic-failure prevention.
8	
9	\paragraph{Adaptive Computation and Routing.}
10	Adaptive routing traces back to learned halting policies \citep{graves2016act}. Mixture-of-Recursions \citep{mixture-of-recursions-2025} learns token-level recursion depths in the LLM era; concurrent work further diversifies routing granularity: CogRouter \citep{cogrouter-2026} adapts per-step depth, ARES \citep{ares-2026} selects effort levels, and MTRouter \citep{mtrouter-2026} addresses multi-turn settings; in death-dominated environments, the task-type-level binary signal is structurally inaccessible to these step-level approaches (\S\ref{sec:router-diagnostic}).
11	RouteLLM \citep{routellm-2024} learns routers from preference data to select between strong and weak models; cost-optimization routing (FrugalGPT~\citep{frugalgpt-2023}; AutoMix~\citep{automix-2024}) cascades queries across model tiers.

_source line 1598 · 2026-05-23T03:46:57.062624+00:00_

1	\begin{abstract}
2	LLM agent routing (deciding when to rely on internal reasoning versus external tool use) is typically addressed by training routers directly, without first understanding what mechanistic signal drives the routing preference. This absence of diagnostic methods risks building routers atop confounded or trivial signal. We propose an episode-level counterfactual framework that compares pure-strategy outcomes (internal-only vs.\ deterministic-only) to decompose routing signal into its mechanistic components before any router is constructed. Applying this framework to ScienceWorld (11 task types, 245 episodes), we find that 78.9\% of non-tie episode-level routing labels arise from death asymmetry---a finding replicated across three architecturally distinct 7--9B model families, revealing that in catastrophic-failure environments, signal diversity can collapse to a single dominant mechanism. Cross-environment probes on MATH and ALFWorld diagnose qualitatively different routing mechanisms (no extractable signal and information asymmetry, respectively), providing preliminary evidence of applicability beyond death-dominated settings. The framework distinguishes \emph{methodological generality} (applicable to any environment with paired episode outcomes) from \emph{empirical generality} (full quantitative validation on ScienceWorld only). A ${\sim}$50-episode pilot suffices for build-vs-buy decisions given large effect sizes ($d = 3.02$).
3	\end{abstract}
4

1	\section{Conclusion}
2	
3	Our episode-level counterfactual framework reveals that 78.9\% of non-tie routing labels in ScienceWorld stem from death avoidance---replicated across three model families, persistent under $10{\times}$ scaling, and transferable to qualitatively different environments. Future work should validate this in additional catastrophic-failure settings and investigate lightweight death-prediction mechanisms that exploit task-type-level binary structure without full step-level router training.
4

40	Our primary analysis uses a single environment (ScienceWorld, 11 of 30 task types) with one backbone (Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct), though extended validation of representative excluded tasks confirms the pattern (Appendix~\ref{app:extended-validation}).   "text": "## Reviewer 1 — 完整审稿意见 (Round 1)\n\n**论文**: \"Death Avoidance as the Dominant Routing Signal: A Counterfactual Analysis on ScienceWorld\"\n**Venue**: EMNLP 2026 via ACL Rolling Review (ARR)\n\n---\n\n## Summary\n\n本文提出了一个 episode-level counterfactual 诊断框架，用于分析 LLM agent routing signal（何时使用内部推理 vs. 外部工具）的构成。将该框架应用于 ScienceWorld（11/30 task types，245 episodes，Qwen2.5-7B），发现 78.9%（75/95）的 non-tie routing labels 源自 death asymmetry——内部策略因幻觉导致的 catastrophic failure 频率远高于 deterministic oracle。Death Avoidance Factor (DAF) 在 P=100 时达 90.4%。跨模型复制（3 families, 7-9B）、agent improvement baselines（Retry, Reflexion, 72B scaling）、跨环境探测（MATH 无信号；ALFWorld 信息不对称）提供了支撑。\n\n---\n\n## Strengths\n\n**S1. 方法论上的 episode-level counterfactual 设计优雅有效。** Per-step oracle 在 sparse-reward 环境下退化为 97.4% 平局率（§2.2），这一观察本身就是方法论贡献。通过在相同初始条件（same random seed）下运行两种 pure strategies，框架干净地隔离了 computation mode 作为唯一变量。\n\n**S2. 透明度和鲁棒性检验极其充分。** 21 个 Appendix (A–U) 涵盖 labeling quality audit (B), cluster bootstrap sensitivity (E), leave-one-task-out (G), seed robustness (J), penalty sensitivity (K), failure mode analysis (N), grounding ablation (O), extended validation (S) 等。这种严谨程度在同类工作中罕见。\n\n**S3. Claims 与证据的校准非常审慎。** 论文明确区分 methodological generality 与 empirical generality（Abstract, §1），将 MATH/ALFWorld 定位为 \"boundary probes\" 而非 cross-domain validation（§3.4），并以 label fraction (78.9%) 而非 DAF (90.4%) 作为 primary penalty-invariant metric（§2.4）。Limitations 部分涵盖 5 个维度，坦诚到位。\n\n**S4. 跨模型复制强劲。** Qwen2.5-7B、Llama-3.1-8B、Gemma-2-9B-it 三个架构不同的模型 family 收敛于 DAF 88–90%（spread 仅 2.2pp），death-prone tasks 方向一致性 10/10（§3.2）。这有力排除了 model-specific 解释。\n\n**S5. Router training negative result 的报告方式值得称赞。** §3.6 对 step-level router 失败提供了两种平衡的解释（noise-dominated labels vs. genuine non-learnability），没有过度解读或回避。\n\n**S6. Oracle degeneracy 观察有力。** Always-deterministic 匹配 96.3% 的 oracle 决策（§3.5），直接表明在 ScienceWorld 中 routing 的 value 极为有限。这是一个重要的实证发现。\n\n---\n\n## Weaknesses\n\n### Must-Fix 问题（影响接收决定）\n\n**N1. [critical] 核心发现的新颖性受限——death dominance 部分是环境设计的数学必然，而非经验发现。**\n- ScienceWorld 施加 P=100 死亡惩罚，internal strategy 因幻觉进入灾难状态死亡率 42.9%（vs. oracle 16.3%）。在 P=100 >> 典型 non-death 分数差（Δ_nd≈2.84）的条件下，death 支配 score gap 是解析可预测的。\n- 论文承认这一点（§2.4: \"DAF increases monotonically with P, a mathematical consequence rather than an empirical finding\"），并使用 penalty-invariant label fraction 作为 primary metric。但 78.9% label fraction 本身也部分是 Algorithm 1 的设计后果：death-priority rule *保证*任何单方死亡 episode 被分类为 death-determined。因此 78.9% 本质上测量的是 \"多少比例的 episodes 恰好一方策略死亡\"——比框架所暗示的更简单的统计量。\n- **影响**: 论文将概念贡献定位为 \"decomposing routing signal into mechanistic components\"，但 decomposition 本质上可归结为一个 death-rate asymmetry 的度量。DAF、SCI 等概念可能构成 methodological overengineering。\n- **建议**: (1) 在 Introduction 中更直接地将 78.9% 描述为 \"death-rate asymmetry 的频率\"，减少 framework 的认知负荷；(2) 强化论文对 \"为什么事先不可预测\" 的论证（当前 §5 \"Non-triviality and oracle confound\" 部分有讨论但不够突出——特别是 72B 不减少 DAF 和 Reflexion 产生 DAF>100% 的发现确实 non-trivial，应更早呈现）。\n- 具体位置：§2.4, §3.1, §5, Abstract\n\n**N2. [critical] 诊断与处方之间的实际差距限制了影响力。**\n- 论文明确声明是 diagnostic 而非 prescriptive（Limitations）。但从诊断中得出的实际建议——\"在 death-dominated 环境中使用 always-deterministic\"——是平凡的，不需要本框架来推导。\n- 更核心的问题是：oracle margin 仅为 2.89 分（Table 2），意味着完美 router 几乎不超过 always-deterministic。这表明在 ScienceWorld 中 routing 本身 low-value，削弱了 routing signal 分析的动机。\n- Practitioner Decision Framework（Appendix R, Table 20）有用但薄弱——4 个条件基于 DAF 阈值（50%, 75%），但这些阈值看起来 arbitrary（无 sensitivity analysis）。\n- **影响**: EMNLP 读者期望 diagnostic 论文提供 actionable insight。当前版本中，最有价值的 actionable finding 是 \"50-episode pilot 足以做 build-vs-buy 决策\"（Appendix R），但这被埋在 appendix 中。\n- **建议**: (1) 将 Decision Framework 从 Appendix R 提升到正文 Discussion 中；(2) 更深入讨论 oracle degeneracy（§3.5）的实际意义——routing 在 ScienceWorld 中价值极低本身是一个重要发现；(3) 明确讨论：如果 death 是可预测的（Failure Mode Analysis 显示所有死亡源自 `focus on` action），那么一个简单的 action-level filter 是否足以替代 router？\n- 具体位置：§3.5, §5, Appendix R\n\n### Should-Fix 问题（影响评分但不一票否决）\n\n**W1. [warning] Cluster bootstrap K=11 低于推荐阈值，影响不确定性量化的可靠性。**\n- K=11 task-type clusters 远低于 Cameron et al. (2008) 和 Webb (2023) 推荐的 K≥20–50。Cluster-robust CI [73.3%, 100.0%] vs. non-clustered [81.1%, 99.0%]。\n- Cohen's d=3.02 是极端效应量（社会科学 >99.9th percentile）——这很可能反映 task-type 内的高度相关二值结构（10/11 death-prone types 全部 100% death-asymmetry labels），而非 genuinely massive effect。论文应更明确地 contextualize 这一数值。\n- 论文提供了多种 supplementary analyses（non-clustered bootstrap, directional consistency, seed replication）来缓解这一问题，这是好的。但应更清楚地引导读者以 non-clustered CI 为 recommended primary inference。\n- 具体位置：§3.1, Limitations (Statistical limitations)\n- 建议：在 §3.1 中添加一句明确推荐：\"Given K=11 < 20, we recommend the non-clustered bootstrap CI [81.1%, 99.0%] as the primary uncertainty estimate.\"\n\n**W2. [warning] 72B scaling 证据不足以支撑 \"persistent under 10× scaling\" 的 claim。**\n- 仅测试了 Qwen2.5-72B（单一模型、单一 family）。无 Llama 或 Gemma 的大规模对应版本。一个数据点无法建立 scaling trend。\n- DAF 从 90.4% 增到 92.4%——但未报告 72B DAF 的 bootstrap CI。这一 2pp 变化在统计上可能不显著。\n- Conclusion 和 Abstract 中的 \"persistent under 10× scaling\" 基于 single model replication，过于绝对。\n- 具体位置：§3.3, Conclusion, Abstract\n- 建议：降低 claim 强度，改为 \"a preliminary 72B probe suggests persistence\" 或类似措辞。\n\n**W3. [warning] Router training negative result 可能被 extreme class imbalance 混淆，而非反映 fundamental non-learnability。**\n- 90.5% 的 non-tie labels 为 deterministic-preferred（86/95），仅 9 个 internal-preferred episodes。标准学习理论预测在 90%+ class imbalance 和 n=95 下 near-chance performance。\n- 论文讨论了这一点（§5: \"distinguishing the two requires ~1,350 paired episodes\"），但当前证据无法分离 data limitation 和 signal limitation 两种解释。\n- §3.6 中 interpretation B（non-learnability）的论证依赖于 text augmentation 无改善和 LOTO 退化到 chance——但前者可能是 augmentation 方法的问题，后者是 task-type-level constant signal 的预期行为（并不能区分 data vs. signal limitation）。\n- 具体位置：§3.6, §5\n- 建议：更明确地将 negative result frame 为 \"inconclusive under current data regime\"，避免倾向 interpretation B。\n\n**W4. [warning] 跨环境探测提供的框架泛化性证据有限。**\n- MATH：84.4% no-preference，无 routing signal——仅确认框架在无信号时不产生误报，但未正面验证 decomposition 能力。\n- ALFWorld：仅 35（Qwen）和 16（Llama）个 non-tie labels。样本量太小，对 \"information asymmetry\" 的刻画信心有限。\n- 两个环境都未展示框架将 routing signal decompose 为 *多个* contributing mechanisms 的能力——这才是 decomposition capability 的最强测试。\n- 具体位置：§3.4\n- 建议：明确这一限制。如果能找到一个同时存在 death avoidance 和 non-death routing signal 的环境（如修改 ScienceWorld 降低 P 或增加 non-death 信号），将大幅增强框架的说服力。\n\n### 小问题（不影响接收）\n\n- §2.4 的 approximation residual (0.23, 0.8%) 讨论了何时近似成立，但未讨论何时会 break down。如果 s_surv 不远小于 P（如软惩罚环境），近似误差会如何变化？\n- §3.1 中 61.2% episodes 为 no-preference——大多数 episodes 不区分策略。框架的 findings 由 38.8%（95 episodes）驱动。这一 base rate 应在正文中更突出讨论。\n- Table 2（§3.5）的 Trained Router 结果（-10.70）用了 single seed (42)，而其他 baselines 用了 full episode set。公平性存疑。\n- SCI=0.2147（Appendix A）的解释价值有限——10/11 task types 都有 death dominance 时，低 concentration 是显然的。\n- Figure 1 的 pipeline 图清晰但可以更紧凑。\n- 部分 appendix 内容（如 Appendix T: Agent Prompt Templates）对审稿不是必需的，但对 reproducibility 有价值。\n\n---\n\n## Questions\n\n**Q1.** 如果包含全部 30 种 ScienceWorld task types（含 19 种 excluded），label fraction 如何变化？即使 excluded types 多为 no-preference，是否有 type 产生 non-death non-tie labels 从而降低 78.9%？\n\n**Q2.** Failure Mode Analysis（Appendix N）显示所有 105 次 internal-strategy deaths 都由 `focus on` action 触发。这意味着 \"death avoidance signal\" 可以更精确地描述为 \"focus-on avoidance\"？`focus on` 操作中多大比例导致死亡？简单的 action-level filter 能否替代 router？\n\n**Q3.** Grounding Ablation（Appendix O）显示 action-space filtering 减少 death 19pp。在 grounding 之后，框架的诊断结论是否改变？death 是否仍然 dominate？\n\n**Q4.** Algorithm 1 的 death-priority rule 在二值 catastrophic failure 环境中合理，但 §5 讨论了 graded penalties 的 extension。如果将 death-priority rule 替换为 continuous severity weighting，现有结论是否 robust？\n\n**Q5.** Oracle degeneracy（96.3% 的 oracle 决策匹配 always-deterministic）是否意味着 ScienceWorld 根本不适合作为 routing research 的 testbed？这一发现对论文的 motivation 有何影响？\n\n---\n\n## Missing References\n\n无 critical omissions。相关工作覆盖全面，包含多篇 2026 concurrent work。Minor suggestions:\n- LLM 不确定性估计（如 Kadavath et al., 2022, \"Language models (mostly) know what they know\"）可强化关于何时内部推理 recognize 自身 failure risk 的讨论。\n- MoE 架构中 token-level routing 的学习文献（beyond Switch Transformer/GShard）可为 granularity 讨论提供更多 context。\n\n---\n\n## Scores\n\n### 官方维度打分（ACL ARR Review Form）\n\n| 维度 | 分数 | 理由摘要 |\n|------|------|----------|\n| **Soundness** | 4/5 | Strong: 方法论严谨，counterfactual 设计 sound，exhaustive robustness checks。主要 caveat 在 cluster bootstrap K=11 和 scaling 证据单薄。 |\n| **Excitement** | 3/5 | Interesting: 诊断框架有方法论价值，但核心发现（death dominance）在 catastrophic-failure 环境中部分可预测；limited novelty + limited actionability 制约 excitement。|\n| **Reproducibility** | 4/5 | Mostly reproducible: 所有环境和模型公开可用，Appendix T 提供完整 prompt templates，承诺代码/数据开源。 |\n| **Overall** | 3.5 (Borderline Conference) | 方法论贡献 solid、执行 thorough、跨模型复制 strong，但 (1) 核心发现新颖性受限（环境设计的数学后果 vs. 经验发现），(2) diagnostic-only 无 prescriptive insight，(3) 单环境 quantitative validation。如果作者能在 rebuttal 中加强 novelty 论证（特别是突出 non-trivial 发现如 72B 不减少 DAF、Reflexion DAF>100%）和 actionability（将 Decision Framework 提升到正文），可考虑提升至 4 (Conference)。|\n| **Confidence** | 4/5 | Quite sure: 仔细审查了方法论、统计推断、所有 appendices，熟悉 routing/agent/counterfactual 相关文献。 |\n\n---\n\n## 一句话总结\n\n方法论严谨的诊断框架在 ScienceWorld 上精确量化了 death dominance 对 routing signal 的支配性，但核心发现的新颖性（部分为环境设计的数学后果）和 practical impact（diagnostic-only, 无 actionable prescription）限制了 excitement。\n\n---\n\n## Detailed Comments（逐节）\n\n### Abstract\n- 清晰准确，claim 与证据匹配。\"revealing that in catastrophic-failure environments, signal diversity can collapse to a single dominant mechanism\" 稍显循环——catastrophic-failure 环境中信号被 catastrophic failure 支配是 expected 的。\n\n### Section 1 (Introduction)\n- \"no prior work has systematically decomposed what drives routing preferences\" 是好的 motivation。97.4% per-step tie rate 的观察有效地推动了 episode-level 分析。\n- Paragraph 2 的 related work positioning（Toolformer, ART, Chameleon, CogRouter, ARES, etc.）简洁到位。\n- Contributions 总结清晰，label fraction 和 DAF 同时呈现，但未在此处说明哪个是 primary（在 §2.4 才阐明）。建议在此处就指明 78.9% label fraction 是 primary metric。\n\n### Section 2 (Method)\n- §2.1: Problem setup 形式化干净。两种 computation mode 的定义清晰。\n- §2.2: Per-step degeneracy (97.4%) 的发现是重要的方法论贡献。\n- §2.3: Algorithm 1 的 death-priority rule 在 binary catastrophic failure 下合理。但 §2.3 正文仅简述 labeling 逻辑，完整 algorithm 在 Appendix B——对理解论文核心方法需翻到 page 13，reading flow 稍受影响。\n- §2.4: DAF 定义清晰，近似 residual (0.8%) 的报告增加可信度。\"Relationship between DAF and label fraction\" 段落是必要的但应更早出现。\"Scope of episode-level analysis\" 段落有效预防了 \"为什么不用 step-level\" 的质疑。\n\n### Section 3 (Experiments)\n- §3.1: Prescreening (11/30 types) 的 justification 充分，anti-cherry-picking analysis (Appendix S) 有力。\n- §3.1: 150/245 (61.2%) no-preference 是重要的 base rate——意味着大多数 episodes 两种策略表现相同。这削弱了 routing 在 ScienceWorld 中的整体 value。建议在正文更突出讨论。\n- §3.2: 跨模型复制是论文最强的贡献之一。DAF 88-90% 的窄幅收敛令人信服。\n- §3.3: Reflexion DAF>100% 的解释清晰（self-correction 在 non-death episodes 略优于 oracle，但 death gap 仍在）。72B 的 DAF 增加（90.4%→92.4%）因 deterministic 也变更安全，是 insightful observation。\n- §3.4: MATH 和 ALFWorld 作为 boundary probes 定位准确。ALFWorld 的 non-tie labels 数量太少（35, 16）是客观限制。\n- §3.5: Oracle degeneracy (96.3%) 应在论文中获得更多 attention——这是一个 striking finding，直接影响对 routing value 的判断。\n- §3.6: Router training diagnostic 的呈现密度高。建议添加 summary table 列出所有 router configurations 和结果。两种 interpretation 的平衡讨论值得肯定。\n\n### Section 4 (Related Work)\n- 全面且组织良好。Safe RL 连接合适但可扩展——death-avoidance finding 对 constrained optimization in agent training 有直接启示。\n\n### Section 5 (Discussion)\n- \"Non-triviality and oracle confound\" 有效回应了最自然的反对意见（Δ_nd=2.84, 仅占 gap 的 9.6%）。\n- \"Practical implications\" 段过于简短。建议将 Appendix R 的 Decision Framework 内容部分移入此处。\n- 缺少对 Q2（`focus on` as sole death trigger）和 Q3（grounding 后的诊断变化）的正面讨论。\n\n### Limitations\n- 异常全面。5 个 limitation paragraphs 均 substantive 且 honest。\n- \"Pure-strategy upper bound\" 观点重要：DAF=90.4% 是 mixed routing 下 death avoidance 贡献的 upper bound。建议在正文 §5 中也提及。\n\n### Appendices\n- 质量高且组织清晰。Appendix Overview 提供了 21 个 section 的导航。\n- Appendix K (Penalty Sensitivity) 直接回应了 DAF 对 P 依赖的问题，展示完整 DAF curve。\n- Appendix N (Failure Mode Analysis) 提供了 `focus on` 作为唯一 death trigger 的详细证据——这是论文最 actionable 的发现之一，应在正文中更突出。\n- Appendix R (Decision Framework) 如前述，应部分提升至正文。",
